# Tools and integrations

[All projects](../README.md) · [Apps powered by Jev](../apps/README.md) · [Share a tool](../../../CONTRIBUTING.md#add-a-community-project)

Developer tools, libraries, SDKs, integrations, and reference implementations for building with Jev. Each full guide explains how to use the project, what needs adapting, and what was checked. For an application with its own user-facing workflow, visit the separate [app directory](../apps/README.md).

## Categories

- [Browser and computer use](#browser-and-computer-use)
- [Customer feedback and marketing](#customer-feedback-and-marketing)
- [Developer tools](#developer-tools)
- [Games and simulation](#games-and-simulation)
- [Home automation](#home-automation)
- [Independent model research](#independent-model-research)
- [SDKs and integrations](#sdks-and-integrations)
- [Search and retrieval](#search-and-retrieval)

## Browser and computer use

See the [computer-use guide](../../../docs/computer-use.md) for a comparison, form/extraction examples, native-app testing designs, and the evidence boundary around iOS demonstrations.

| Project | What you can do | Stack / format |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [@sideform/browser-agent](sideform-browser-agent.md) | TypeScript browser agent library; step policy decides with a System One client (Jev). | TypeScript · library (MIT) |
| [Agent Browser (caglarkc)](caglarkc-agent-browser.md) | Research browser runtime for AI agents: observes pages through Lightpanda DOM controls, lets Jev choose the next action, and verifies outcomes independently, with reproducible benchmarks. | Node.js research prototype (CLI + lab) (MIT) |
| [agent-desktop](agent-desktop.md) | Drive macOS apps via accessibility refs; optional jev-desktop skill/scripts ask TypeSafe Jev for target/command without putting the a11y tree in agent context (CLI works without Jev). | Rust · CLI/npm (`agent-desktop` 0.9.2) + Node jev scripts |
| [agy-cua (background computer use for Antigravity)](agy-cua.md) | Unofficial MCP plugin that gives Google Antigravity (app and `agy` CLI) background computer use on macOS through the open-source Cua Driver, with an optional Jev lane that picks each guarded step in about 0.2 to 0.3 s and refuses secure fields and send or commit actions. | Antigravity plugin with stdio MCP servers and a bash CLI (macOS) (MIT) |
| [ajevt-browser](ajevt-browser.md) | Bounded System One browser loop for Pi/OpenCode/Amp/MCP via agent-browser (observe→Jev→act). | TypeScript · npm (`ajevt-browser` / `ajevt-browser-mcp`, AGPL-3.0) |
| [android-jev (FZ2000)](fz2000-android-jev.md) | Drive an Android phone over adb via MCP; TypeSafe Jev chooses each next action (distinct from jev-android/jevdevice). | Python · MCP server + skill (MIT) |
| [brojev](brojev.md) | Go browser agent for Claude Code with its own CDP engine, where Jev (via OpenRouter) picks each action and target during a delegated browser sub-task. | Claude Code plugin + Go binary (MIT) |
| [ck-browser (plain-language browser checks)](ck-browser.md) | Runs browser checks written as plain-language steps in a real Chrome, with Jev picking each element from a DOM snapshot and deciding whether the page has settled, and writes per-step probabilities, timings, cost and screenshots. | CLI (Python 3.11+ standard library only) (MIT) |
| [CloakBrowser-Agent](cloakbrowser-agent.md) | Stealth browser agent: TypeSafe Jev decides each step; CloakBrowser executes (MCP/CLI/Python). | Python · MCP/CLI/library (MIT) |
| [Cua jev-use](cua-jev-use.md) | Compose a bounded chooser with Driver and independent fixture verification. | Python / TypeScript · integration recipe |
| [CUA-JEV (ZJU-REAL)](cua-jev-zju.md) | Constrained computer-use loop: Jev selects typed action×channel candidates with guards and verifiers. | Python · framework (Apache-2.0) |
| [DepthJev](depthjev.md) | Embodied navigation with depth/text facts; TypeSafe Jev chooses EB-Navigation actions. | Python · EmbodiedBench agent (Apache-2.0) |
| [Discord Policy Checker](discord-policy-checker.md) | Hold Discord sends that break your plain-language policies, judged by Jev. | TypeScript · Chrome extension (MIT) |
| [Dowse (semantic find for Safari)](dowse.md) | macOS app and Safari extension that opens from the MacBook notch: describe a passage you remember, and Jev finds and highlights the matching text on the page so you can step through matches. | macOS app and Safari extension (Swift, TypeScript); developer build (MIT) |
| [dsh-opencli](dsh-opencli.md) | DeepSeek Harness (dsh) plugin that drives your own logged-in Chrome or Edge through OpenCLI: 180+ site commands, generic browser primitives, approval gates on writes, watch jobs, and a System One decision layer (Jev or local Laya) for fast checks. | dsh plugin (Node.js) (MIT) |
| [ego-decision-layer](ego-decision-layer.md) | Pluggable ego lite browser decision layer: one TypeSafe Jev System One call per step with fail-closed guards (measured benches). | JavaScript · skill/CLI (MIT) |
| [ego-jev](ego-jev.md) | Let Jev choose clicks and field placement in ego lite browser TaskSpaces while your agent keeps the goal. | JavaScript · Node CLI (MIT) |
| [fast-browser](fast-browser.md) | Playwright browser automation for Codex/MCP with TypeSafe Jev (or Laya) decisions | Python · browser automation / MCP (MIT) |
| [firefox-jev-mcp](firefox-jev-mcp.md) | Claude plans; TypeSafe Jev picks Firefox element actions via MCP + WebExtension. | TypeScript · MCP + Firefox extension (MIT) |
| [Flick (flick-computer-use)](flick-computer-use.md) | MCP computer-use: Jev decides browser/macOS actions for whole goals. | TypeScript · MCP (MIT) |
| [Footwork](footwork.md) | Dual-process browser agent: TypeSafe Jev as System 1 in front of browser-use System 2, with a code-owned arbiter and evidence verification. | Python/Rust · package (`jevdual` 0.0.1) |
| [glimpse](glimpse-jev.md) | Computer-use harness for the Linux GNOME desktop: a planner model writes the plan, and Jev finds each control, checks each result and decides when the task is done, across LibreOffice, Files, the browser and other apps. | Linux desktop agent (Python) (PolyForm-Noncommercial-1.0.0 (source available, not OSI open source)) |
| [gpui-agent](gpui-agent.md) | Drive instrumented GPUI apps via accessibility: TypeSafe Jev chooses typed actions/targets (no screenshots to the model). | Rust/Python/TypeScript · experimental native toolkit |
| [HN Jev Sorter](hn-jev-sorter.md) | Sort Hacker News comments into custom buckets live with Jev. | JavaScript · Chrome extension (MIT) |
| [InspireJev](inspire-jev.md) | Hand repetitive public-website steps (search, filter, collect, fill forms) to Jev-chosen actions while the host agent keeps the goal, authorization and verification. | Node.js · Playwright · release tarball (MIT) |
| [IronBee Express](ironbee-express.md) | Fast, cheap browser task runs with Jev choosing among offered controls, plus a post-run review. | TypeScript · CLI + local web UI (Elastic License 2.0) |
| [Jev Ad Block](jev-adblock.md) | Chrome MV3 ad blocker that asks Jev to judge candidate page elements. | TypeScript · Chrome extension (GPL-3.0) |
| [Jev Browser (openqa-cn)](openqa-jev-browser.md) | Indexed Playwright automation: TypeSafe Jev chooses control/op; replay, generate, explore, HTML reports (CodexQA skill). | TypeScript · CLI (`codexqa-jev-browser` 0.1.0, MIT) |
| [Jev Browser (tontoko)](jev-browser-tontoko.md) | Fill forms, extract records with evidence, and add semantic selection to Playwright tests. | TypeScript · SDK, CLI and MCP |
| [Jev Browser (Ying-Kai-Liao)](jev-browser-ying-kai-liao.md) | Run small browser goals with Jev action/target selection and direct inspection; page data and supplied values reach TypeSafe. | JavaScript · Playwright library, CLI and MCP |
| [Jev Browser Agent](jev-browser-agent.md) | Bun and TypeScript browser agent with an indexed action space: a typed decision model (Jev, local or cloud) picks the operation and element each step, a chat helper writes a checklist, and a live inspector streams Chromium as it works. | Bun app + TypeScript library (MIT) |
| [Jev Browser MCP (saurabhgangurde)](jev-browser-mcp-saurabh.md) | Local MCP server that lets a coding agent run bounded browser tasks in a dedicated Chrome window: `browser_task` follows a goal through Jev-selected page actions drawn only from the request and live page evidence, with the full Chrome DevTools MCP operation set also exposed. | MCP server (TypeScript, Node.js 22+) (MIT) |
| [Jev Browser Skill](jev-browser-skill.md) | Learn a minimal Jev-driven browser loop as a Claude Code/Codex skill (reference; see Ultrafast for fuller agents). | Agent skill + CDP scripts (`scripts/*.mjs`); explainer site |
| [Jev Chromium](jev-chromium.md) | Electron browser agent that collects data across pages: Jev picks each click, focus, navigation or done step from the page's actionable elements, an OpenAI-compatible text model plans, fills and verifies, and results export as a cited table or CSV. | Electron desktop app (Node.js); README mainly in Chinese (MIT) |
| [Jev kit by Mr-heka (record browser or Mac app chores and replay them with Jev)](heka-jev-kit.md) | Skill for Claude Code and Codex: the agent records a browser or Mac app chore into a recipe once, then a runner replays it at machine speed, sending only fuzzy calls (right page, right result, which option fits) to Jev through OpenRouter. | Agent skill with Python scripts (MIT) |
| [JEV OpenFast Browser](jev-openfast-browser.md) | Local chat-driven browser agent built on Jev Ultrafast: Jev (via OpenRouter) picks each operation and target from a numbered element table in a real Chrome tab, checks results before reporting success, and hands over for logins or CAPTCHAs. | Python app (`uv`) with local chat UI (beta) (MIT) |
| [Jev Ultrafast](jev-ultrafast.md) | Select browser operations and targets from the current page. | Python · browser agent and inspector |
| [Jev Voice Browser](jev-voice-browser.md) | Study partial speech, target disambiguation, and browser actions with an inspectable decision policy. | JavaScript · Playwright voice-control reference |
| [jev-adb](jev-adb.md) | MCP server: Android control over ADB with Jev typed UI decisions and safety gates. | TypeScript · MCP server (MIT) |
| [jev-android](jev-android.md) | Drive Android UI via accessibility with TypeSafe Jev or DeepSeek action choice (Kotlin SDK + sample). | Kotlin · Android SDK (`core`/`sdk`/`sample` 0.2.0) |
| [jev-apply](jev-apply.md) | Fill job applications from your own data; Laya or Jev picks the fact/option per field. | Python · Playwright agent (MIT) |

| [jev-browse (danielnc)](danielnc-jev-browse.md) | Fast Jev browser sub-tasks on browser-harness for coding agents. | Python · harness (MIT) |

| [jev-browser-sidekick-mcp](jev-browser-sidekick-mcp.md) | Run plain-language browser steps via MCP; Jev picks the control; pairs with agentic-playwright-mcp for a shared Chrome session. | TypeScript · npm MCP (`jev-browser-sidekick-mcp` / `jev-bro`) |
| [jev-browser-skill (ChenYCL)](chenycl-jev-browser-skill.md) | Browser/computer-use CLI+MCP: TypeSafe Jev action choice (ego lite/Chrome/Safari); distinct from zurfyx/hqman. | Node.js ≥ 22 · CLI/MCP skill (MIT) |
| [jev-browser-skill (hqman)](hqman-jev-browser-skill.md) | Playwright Chromium skill/`jb` CLI: Jev chooses in-page actions (Gateway or TypeSafe); distinct from zurfyx teaching skill. | Node.js ≥ 22.18 · skill + CLI (`jb`, Apache-2.0) |

| [jev-sim-use](jev-sim-use.md) | Jev-speed mobile UI navigation on sim-use (iOS/Android). | CLI · mobile (MIT) |

| [jev-ultrafast-skill (SherseaHe)](sherseahe-jev-ultrafast-skill.md) | Agent Skill package for bounded Jev Ultrafast Chrome automation (install via `npx skills add`); not the upstream runtime. | Python · Agent Skill + runner (MIT) |
| [jevb](jevb.md) | Let an agent drive a browser or phone with plain-English steps, with Jev choosing elements. | JavaScript · Node CLI (MIT) |

| [picode-decisions-jev (Jev for COGNIX[WS])](picode-decisions-jev.md) | COGNIX[WS] extension that lets TypeSafe Jev answer the agents' fast browser decisions, such as which page element matches a description and yes/no checks about a page, so agents act without reading the whole page each step. | COGNIX[WS] extension (Node.js, no dependencies) (Apache-2.0) |
| [plain](plain.md) | Describe targets by role and visible text and assertions as claims; Jev picks elements and judges claims, with lock files and run modes to keep runs repeatable. | TypeScript · npm `@gabe4coding/plain` + Claude Code / Codex plugins (MIT) |
| [PlayJev (filed)](filedcom-playjev.md) | Add `page.act(...)`, `page.check(...)` style natural-language steps to Playwright tests and scripts, with Jev choosing from numbered page nodes. | TypeScript · npm `@filed/playjev` + Playwright (MIT, experimental) |
| [QAJev](qajev.md) | Run goal-driven exploratory tests with Jev choosing actions from the page text, separating product failures from harness problems, with HTML/MD/JSON reports and a live dashboard. | Python · CLI `qajev` + MCP (MIT) |
| [quick-qa-helper](quick-qa-helper.md) | Android UI QA loop for coding agents: a planner model picks the next action, TypeSafe Jev picks the target element (Box on a screenshot when the UI tree lacks it) and independently verifies done, adb acts, and you get a pass/fail report with one summary image. | Python CLI + on-device server + agent skill (MIT) |
| [refsure (browser judgment for coding agents)](refsure.md) | CLI and agent skill that coding agents in Orca terminals or next to an agent-browser session call to answer browser questions they often get wrong (which control means X, how to pass a banner, whether a click landed), using Jev decisions over a hidden page in the agent's own tab. | CLI and agent skill (TypeScript, Node.js) (MIT) |
| [REPLAY](replay-jev.md) | Fast/slow agent split with Jev judging each screen and demonstrations replayed as task chains. | JavaScript (Node) + Swift OCR · CLI and skill (MIT) |
| [Reverie](reverie.md) | Run Markdown browser test specs where Jev (or local Laya) decides each click. | Python · CLI agent (MIT) |
| [Screen Intelligence (desk)](screen-intelligence-desk.md) | macOS command-line "hands and eyes" for Claude Code: Claude writes a plan and Jev does the looking and clicking, using the accessibility tree first and a two-pass Box search on pixels second; 47 of 48 buttons found in about 0.6 s each, and it stops before send, submit, delete or pay. | Python command-line tool (`desk`) for Claude Code on macOS (MIT) |
| [Sedum](sedum.md) | Mix plain-English steps with ordinary Playwright code; Jev returns probabilities for element resolution and claim checks while clicking, waiting, verdicts and exit codes stay deterministic. | TypeScript · npm `sedum-cli` + `@sedum-dev/provider-typesafe` (MIT) |
| [Surf CLI](surf-cli.md) | Drive Chrome from any agent; opt-in `semantic.find` / `semantic.act` let Jev pick the next allowed action and verify the goal (other commands never call TypeSafe). | TypeScript · npm CLI + Chrome extension + native host (MIT) |
| [System One Computer Use (screenshot loop agent driven by Jev)](system-one-computer-use.md) | Prototype computer-use agent in one Python file: it takes a screenshot, asks Jev what to do next (click, type, press Enter, scroll, open a site or stop) and does it with pyautogui; a small web dashboard runs tasks and shows reports. | Python script and dashboard (MIT) |
| [Tacit](tacit.md) | Local-first browser context harness: reads the page and navigation trail on your machine and hands them to Claude Code, Cursor, or any MCP agent, with a TypeSafe Jev-backed DecisionEngine (`core/jev.ts` → `/v1/systemone`). | Bun/TypeScript local app + MCP tools (MIT) |
| [Theme Tab Filter (jev-tab-filter)](jev-tab-filter.md) | Score Chrome tabs against a plain-English theme with TypeSafe Jev, then group/hide/window/close matches. | JavaScript · Chrome MV3 extension (MIT) |
| [tinycomputer](tinyhumansai-tinycomputer.md) | Drive desktop a11y and Chrome via a Rust TinyBus module; flows/tasks ask TypeSafe Jev many small questions (no screenshots). | Rust · TinyBus cdylib module (GPL-3.0) |
| [typesafe-computer-use](typesafe-computer-use.md) | Study OCR and Accessibility driven native macOS control. | Python · desktop CLI |
| [typesafe-computer-use-win](typesafe-computer-use-win.md) | Study OCR/UI Automation driven native Windows control with TypeSafe Jev decisions (`winclicker`). | Python · Windows desktop CLI |
| [winuse](winuse.md) | MCP server and CLI that lets an AI observe and operate Windows desktop apps through UI Automation, WebView2, input, windows, processes and files, with Jev used for grounding and verification checks, and saved scenarios you can replay as end-to-end tests. | MCP server + CLI (.NET 10, Windows) (MIT) |
| [x-stamp (Jev labels for X posts)](x-stamp.md) | Chrome extension that stamps each X post as it scrolls into view with what it is for (news, hot take, builder ad, engagement bait and more) and what it is about, using Jev to pick the type and quote the post's own words. | Chrome extension (unpacked, no build) (MIT) |

News Japan article cards by keyword and, optionally, uses Jev to highlight articles related to your interests and hide ones related to topics you don't want, with a details table of each verdict. | Chrome extension (TypeScript); README in Japanese (MIT) |

## Customer feedback and marketing

| Project | What you can do | Stack / format |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [AI PM Job Radar](ai-pm-job-radar.md) | Every open PM role at 36 AI and tech companies, sorted by Jev (via Vercel AI Gateway) into AI PM without ML, AI PM with ML, non-AI PM and not-a-PM-role. | Node.js pipeline + Next.js site (MIT) |
| [AnchorLint](anchorlint.md) | Audit internal links in built HTML: deterministic checks plus optional TypeSafe Jev promise/relevance judgments. | Python · CLI (`anchorlint`) |
| [call-eval](call-eval.md) | Score calls for satisfaction (1–5) and sentiment with an audit trail of transcript lines per number; runs offline or live against Jev. | Python · CLI package + static demo site (MIT) |
| [Clay JEV People Ranker](clay-jev-people-ranker.md) | Qualify Clay people-search candidates with TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul before enrichment (Agent Skill + Python script). | Python · Agent Skill + CLI script (`rank_clay_people.py`) |
| [Competitor Monitor with Jev (Mastra template)](competitor-monitor-jev.md) | Official Mastra template that watches competitor pricing, changelog, and docs pages, diffs them against saved history, has TypeSafe Jev classify each change, and applies a policy to ignore, record, request review, or alert, with an LLM explaining results. | Mastra (TypeScript) template (Apache-2.0) |
| [discourse-chatbot](discourse-chatbot.md) | The original Discourse AI Chatbot plugin; optional System One settings (default model `jev-latest`) let Jev decide whether a question belongs to the community and which tools the bot should use. | Discourse plugin (Ruby) (GPL-3.0) |
| [Founder CRM](founder-crm.md) | Local SQLite CRM for an early startup's first customers, conversations and promises, with a dashboard, Claude over MCP to propose updates, and optional Jev checks on whether the evidence supports each proposed change before you approve it. | Python app (SQLite dashboard + MCP) (MIT) |
| [Gatekeeper Editorial 30X](gatekeeper-30x.md) | Hackathon web app (in Spanish) where Jev answers typed questions about each content piece and code routes it to auto-approve, LLM rewrite, human review or reject; a second flow sorts Luma event sign-ups into programs and decides whether to call them now. | Next.js 16 web app (TypeScript) with a hosted demo (AGPL-3.0) |
| [Jev Content Moderation](jev-content-mod.md) | Content moderation demo with asymmetric approve / shadow / remove thresholds on Jev probabilities, so irreversible removal needs more certainty than approval. | Python demo (uv) (MIT) |
| [Jev Internal Links](jev-internal-links.md) | Claude Code skill: crawl sitemap paragraphs, TypeSafe Jev picks useful internal link targets, local rules + report. | Python · Claude Code skill (MIT) |

## Games and simulation

| Project | What you can do | Stack / format |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [2048 with Jev](2048-with-jev.md) | 2048 experiment where a bot chooses moves via the TypeSafe SDK (`typesafe-sdk`, pinned) using a fixed Jev model id so runs do not silently follow `jev-latest` alias moves. | Python 2048 bot + game UI pieces (MIT) |
| [2048: Jev vs Laya](2048-jev-vs-laya.md) | Reproducible 2048 head-to-head of Jev vs Laya with move-level logs. | Python · simulation + reports (MIT) |
| [Agent Chess (Claude vs Jev)](agent-chess.md) | Local chess board styled after chess.com where Claude models and Jev play each other, with per-side engine settings, Jev move probabilities in the live view, a per-run spend cap and server-side legality checks. | Local web app (Node.js) (MIT) |
| [AGNES Arena (Pokémon battles with Jev-assisted decisions)](agnes-arena.md) | Pokémon Showdown battle arena where two persistent agent players evaluate legal moves through Jev, commit an action and play whole matches, with session management, a scheduler and a browser battle viewer. | Node.js workspace (four packages) (MIT) |
| [argmax](argmax.md) | Unity swarm-programming RTS where four commanders, including TypeSafe Jev answering typed battlefield questions, emit the same decision type. | Unity 6 game + SpacetimeDB server (C#) (MIT) |
| [banterarena-engine](banterarena-engine.md) | Make a social-banter scoring formula public and testable, with Jev judging only the ambiguous replies. | Go · library (MIT, no third-party dependencies) |
| [Chicken Bench (decision APIs playing Crossy Road)](chicken-bench.md) | Playful benchmark of how far, fast and cheaply decision APIs can play Crossy Road, comparing jev-latest and jev-preview with GPT and Clef models on score, latency, tokens and cost per game, with best-run videos. | Python benchmark (uv) (MIT) |
| [Choice Four](choice-four.md) | Four-in-a-row against Jev: the rules engine finds legal moves and tactical facts, and every Jev turn is a structured Choice over the remaining columns, with server-authoritative matches, Discord sign-in, leaderboards and a hash-linked move journal. | Node.js game server + browser UI (also deployable as a Cloudflare Worker) (MIT) |
| [Classifier Arena](classifier-arena.md) | Three small browser games (a real-time fighter, a spam and smishing lane game, and a reverse-dictionary clue game) where Jev and OpenAI Decisions play head to head on one shared classifier core, with every match logged and replayable. | TypeScript browser games and match runner (Node 24, pnpm) (MIT) |
| [Conjure (decision model art-directs Three.js scenes)](conjure.md) | Type an idea or mood and a decision model picks worlds, things, looks and directions from a catalogue of procedural Three.js components, then builds and prints the scene in seconds; works with Jev directly or other System One hosts. | Local web app (Node.js, Three.js) (MIT) |
| [CourtVision (basketball computer vision with a Jev decision layer)](courtvision.md) | Real-time basketball pipeline that turns single-camera video into verified stats, then uses Jev to classify play type and moment stakes, rank coach cards and guard Claude's narration lines, with an operator dashboard over WebSockets. | Python library and CLI (MIT) |
| [Deadline Tetris](deadline-tetris.md) | Live benchmark where Jev, Clef, Laya, Strands Decider and LLMs play the same Tetris game while pieces keep falling: over four games Jev landed about 119 pieces per game with a 289 ms median answer and won all four, while slower models placed better moves but too late. | Bun + TypeScript web app with model adapters and recorded runs (MIT) |
| [Dernier Abri (Last Shelter)](dernier-abri.md) | French student 3D browser defence game where Jev estimates from a text description whether a night silhouette is a zombie or a survivor, and deterministic, tested code decides whether to fire, using Bayesian evidence fusion and a sequential test. | Python server and browser 3D game (MIT) |
| [Doom in Claude Code (Jev pilots)](doom-in-claude-code.md) | Claude Code plugin that runs ViZDoom with Freedoom in a terminal pane: Jev looks at the game screen and picks the strategy about 2.5 times a second, Claude Haiku narrates in Spanish or English, and Python reflexes and an A* navigator handle aiming and the map. | Claude Code plugin (Python bridge) (MIT) |
| [Drone Autonomy: AI vs Rules](drone-autonomy-ai-vs-rules.md) | Local benchmark that flies a simulated Crazyflie drone through adversarial events and measures collision rate against decision latency for Jev, other decision models, chat LLMs, rule-based pilots and a perfect oracle. | Python simulator + dashboard (MIT) |
| [gm-autoplayer](gm-autoplayer.md) | Agent that plays browser games such as slither.io from screenshots through the Playwright MCP server; pick an LLM brain, a Jev brain that answers quick typed questions about each frame (danger, nearest food) for game rules to turn into moves, or a hybrid with an LLM strategist. | Python project (uv) driving Playwright MCP (MIT) |
| [Godot Jev (Godot 4 addon wrapping the Jev System One API)](godot-jev.md) | Godot 4.7+ addon that exposes Jev as an autoload: choice, score, noul and box questions about game state, batched in one request, with native Godot inputs such as Dictionary, Image and Texture2D. | Godot 4 addon (GDScript) (MIT) |
| [godot-jev-actions (Jev action nodes for Godot 4)](godot-jev-actions.md) | Godot 4 addon with designer-facing Jev nodes: an ActionCatalog allowlist, an ActionPicker that turns a sentence or game state into one allowed action with arguments, and a FreeTextChoice that maps typed text to an authored choice. Nothing is generated. | Godot 4 addon (GDScript) (MIT) |
| [Gomoku × Jev](gomoku-jev.md) | Five-in-a-row against Jev on a Three.js 3D board (README in Chinese): local heuristics propose up to ten tactically labelled moves and Jev picks one with a Choice question, showing the pick and its confidence in the status bar. | Vite + Three.js web game with a server-side proxy for the key (MIT) |
| [Hammertime](hammertime.md) | Local live Formula 1 strategy desk: a strategy engine owns the numbers (tyre model, pit loss, Monte Carlo), while System One models such as TypeSafe Jev read race-control messages, gauge rain risk, and weigh evidence into box-now or stay-out calls. | Python app + local dashboard (MIT) |

| [Honeytongue](honeytongue.md) | NPC persuasion engine: TypeSafe Jev judges whether player dialogue convinced a character. | JavaScript · library (MIT) |

| [JevPilot](jevpilot.md) | Inspect sampled driving paths, Jev choices and local braking in a browser simulation; application licensing is unspecified. | JavaScript / Three.js · simulation demo |
| [JevPokerBench](jev-poker-bench.md) | Compare official Jev and other agents on Texas Hold'em (cash/SNG boards, replay, BYOK tables). | Python/React · FastAPI playground (`pokerbench` 0.1.0) |
| [jevSociety (social feed simulation judged by Jev)](jev-society.md) | Agent-based simulation of 10,000 social-media accounts in which Jev is the only judge of which opinions fit together, asked live for every like and change of mind, to compare a feed ranked by predicted likes against a random feed. | Research simulation with web viewer (Python) (MIT) |
| [Jong (Pong against Jev)](jong.md) | Pong in the browser against an AI paddle steered by Jev's typed decisions, fetched live during a best-of-3 match, with Jev's response time shown in the HUD. | Browser game (canvas) with a small Node.js proxy (Apache-2.0) |
| [Keeping an NPC on quest](keeping-an-npc-on-quest.md) | Educational NPC demo: staged facts + Jev Choice/Noul checks on every line. | Python · tutorial code (Apache-2.0) |
| [Kettei Kart](kettei-kart.md) | Camera-only SuperTuxKart harness for decision models: each frame returns a probability for each of nine controls and the highest wins, with recorded head-to-head results and adapters for Sqwish's Kettei Fat, Liquid D1 Ultra, and TypeSafe Jev. | Python harness + recording tools (Apache-2.0) |
| [Ministry of Truth](ministry-of-truth.md) | Play (or study) a game whose only actions are your words, judged by one batched Jev call per briefing with an automatic offline fallback. | Vanilla JS · zero-dependency Node server |
| [PokéSurvivor x Jev learning bot](pokesurvivor-jev.md) | Experimental bot for the browser survival game PokéSurvivor: a Chrome extension handles 60 FPS dodging locally, and a local bridge asks Jev for level-up cards, items and tactics, learning only from what it sees on screen (README in Cantonese). | Chrome MV3 extension plus local Python bridge (port 8765) (Apache-2.0) |
| [quackd](quackd.md) | Drive multi-robot goals with LLM pilots; optional `--jev` TypeSafe stepper for closed-set verb choices. | Python · CLI (`quackd`) and robot extras |
| [s1doom (System1Doom)](system1doom.md) | Harness where text-only System One models (TypeSafe Jev or open-weight Laya) play DOOM through ViZDoom: code computes geometry, the model makes the judgment. | Python harness (uv) (No LICENSE file) |
| [snake-ai (Snake learning lab)](snake-ai-lab.md) | Hands-on lab and nine-lesson course where neural networks learn Snake with a genetic algorithm or DQN, plus an untrained third player where Jev picks each move from a text description of what the snake senses. | Python lab with web dashboard (PyTorch, numpy); live read-only demo (MIT) |
| [SoulsBench](soulsbench.md) | Compare Jev with other decision models on a realtime game-control benchmark. | Python · benchmark harness + Windows bridge (MIT) |
| [System One Arcade and @system-one/ai](system-one-arcade.md) | Bun workspace with one typed TypeScript API over System One decision providers (TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter, OpenAI, Cloudflare Clef, Vercel AI Gateway) and a React arcade where those models play games against each other while a server logs every call and ranks them. | TypeScript library (`@system-one/ai`) and React arcade app (Bun workspace; Fly.io and Docker deploy notes) (MIT) |
| [system-one-chess](system-one-chess.md) | Play chess against TypeSafe Jev (Gateway/OpenRouter) with Stockfish analysis; optional local Laya. | Python ≥ 3.11 · web/Docker (`system-one-chess` 0.5.0, GPL-3.0) |
| [Thorkell (Valheim companion)](thorkell-valheim.md) | Research project: a Valheim companion that joins your world as a real second player, reads its own game screen, takes chat requests like "make us an axe" or "follow me", and uses Jev for fast typed decisions under a deadline while a larger model plans and talks. | Game agent (Python brain + game client on macOS) (MIT) |
| [TypeSafe Mario](typesafe-mario.md) | Study Jev action choices over emulator telemetry with a synthetic state demo and decision logs; licensing is unspecified. | Python · emulator controller and dashboard |

## Home automation

| Project | What you can do | Stack / format |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Calendar Curator (Home Assistant)](calendar-curator.md) | Home Assistant custom integration that reads events from your calendars, asks Jev whether each one matches a criteria prompt, and mirrors the matches into a target calendar, with caching, reconciliation and a deletion cap. | Home Assistant custom integration (Python, HACS) (MIT) |
| [HA Jev Autopilot](ha-jev-autopilot.md) | Per-room Jev decisions with deterministic HA actions and phone confirmation for risky devices. | Python · Home Assistant integration (MIT) |
| [HA_SystemOne](ha-systemone.md) | Polish-language Home Assistant command layer: local embedding and reranker models narrow about 100 possible actions to five, Jev picks one or none with a confidence, and the action runs only at 0.8 or above; otherwise it asks back. | Python service with Docker Compose and Home Assistant REST integration (MIT) |
| [Jev for Home Assistant](ha-jev.md) | Turn household context into judgment sensors and automation responses. | Python · Home Assistant integration |
| [Laya for Home Assistant](home-assistant-laya.md) | Run a fully local Assist conversation agent on open-weight Laya with speculative intent/entity scoring (independent of hosted Jev). | Python · Home Assistant integration (Apache-2.0) |
| [Typesafe routing proxy (decision fast path for Home Assistant voice)](typesafe-openai-proxy.md) | OpenAI-compatible proxy that sits behind the hass_local_openai_llm Home Assistant integration: one System One decision per request turns clear commands and state questions into Home Assistant tool calls, asks before risky actions, and forwards everything else unchanged to your LLM server. | TypeScript server (Docker image or Node.js) (MIT) |

## Independent model research

These projects study related typed-decision patterns using other models. They are independent research implementations, not official Jev releases or validated substitutes; their guides separate code, model and dataset licensing, and reported benchmarks from catalog checks.

| Project | What you can do | Stack / format |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [8-K Filings and Decision Models](sec-8k-decision-models.md) | Jev rated 26,962 S&P 500 8-K filings (2013 to 2026) from very bad to very good for $2.32; within item group and year the rating's rank correlation with the next two days' market-adjusted return is +0.025, positive in 13 of 14 years, and the move is mostly gone by the next open. | Python research package with report, figures, 402 offline tests and hashed results (MIT) |
| [@amatelic/jev](amatelic-jev.md) | Node library and local server for one-line choice/noul/score decisions from small open models, with no API key or cloud call. | Node.js library + HTTP server (MIT) |
| [A Decision Model as a World Model](jev-world-model.md) | Reproduce a study of Jev as a frozen world model for a TextWorld LLM agent. | Python · research code + artifacts (MIT) |
| [agent-test-pruning](agent-test-pruning.md) | Scripts and data for the paper "Which Tests to Keep When a Coding Agent Writes the Test Suite": replays of Claude Code writing tests, planted and real bugs, and pruning signals including four facet questions asked of Jev per test. | Python scripts + datasets (MIT) |
| [Albanian sentiment analysis with Jev](albanian-sentiment-jev.md) | See how zero-shot Jev compares with trained models on a low-resource-language sentiment benchmark. | Python · research code + results (MIT) |
| [AnyJev (Nokia Applied Research)](nokia-anyjev.md) | Turn an open LLM into Jev-style typed decisions with probabilities (L0–L2); independent of official Jev. | Python · PyPI (`anyjev` 0.1.0, Apache-2.0) |
| [assay](assay.md) | Run local typed Choice/Score/Noul-style decisions with calibrated confidence (assay; independent of hosted Jev). | Python · stdlib local decision engine (MIT) |
| [basal](basal.md) | Self-host Jev-style typed decisions (probability per allowed answer) on your GPU or Mac, and compare against hosted Jev on the maintainer's Werdykt benchmark. | Python · `basal-serve` engine (vLLM/SGLang/MLX/llama.cpp backends) (Apache-2.0) |
| [Beyond CLIP: decision models for industrial image QC](decision-models-industrial-image-qc.md) | Ongoing research study (R code, data and rendered analysis) comparing Jev and the open-weight Clef-Flash with few-shot CLIP for calibrated defect detection on five industrial image datasets, with a five-stage audit, refine, calibrate and freeze framework. | Research repository (R, Quarto analysis) (MIT) |
| [BLADE](blade-failure-attribution.md) | Multi-agent failure attribution research; all audit judgments are Jev multiple-choice questions. | Python · research code (MIT) |
| [Bongard](bongard.md) | Run Bongard open System One judgments (parallel typed questions → probabilities); independent of hosted Jev. | Python · HF weights + inference (Apache-2.0) |
| [Brier](brier.md) | Run a local MLX Jev-format choice/score/noul decision model (PT-BR training focus) on Apple Silicon. | Python · MLX LoRA decision model (Apache-2.0) |
| [browser-laya](browser-laya.md) | Typed System One decisions client-side in the browser with open Laya weights, no API key. | TypeScript · npm `@wexare/laya-web` + Vite playground (MIT) |
| [Bud Decision Studio](bud-decision-studio.md) | Run open Jev-like decision models locally behind a TypeSafe-compatible API, with a desktop UI. | Desktop app + local server (no LICENSE — source available) |
| [Can you fool Jev?](can-you-fool-jev.md) | See where Jev and local decision models break on trick choice, yes/no and score questions, and rerun the same requests against your own endpoint. | Python · dataset + scripts (MIT) |
| [Canopy-Jev](canopy-jev.md) | Run Canopy-Jev tree-based typed decisions with shared context and isolated branches (independent of hosted Jev). | Python · Qwen tree decision research (MIT) |
| [Chinese-Jev](gulucaptain-chinese-jev.md) | Build/fine-tune Chinese typed-decision models with the Chinese-Jev pipeline and CJ-Bench (weights release pending; independent of hosted Jev). | Python · research pipeline (Apache-2.0) |
| [Claims, Choices and Labels (jev-eval)](jev-eval.md) | Read an audit of Jev and five LLMs on Taiwan-status claims, choices and labels. | Node.js · research code + data (MIT / CC BY 4.0) |
| [clef-webcam](clef-webcam.md) | Try image-state System One questions locally on your webcam with open Clef weights (Jev-compatible schema). | Python · FastAPI server + browser page (Apache-2.0) |
| [cleffa](cleffa.md) | Run Cloudflare Clef/Clef-Flash locally on Apple Silicon Metal and serve POST /v1/systemone typed decisions (BF16; text-only v1). | C11 + Metal 4 · clef/clef-server (MIT) |
| [CLM (Contrastive Language Models)](contrastive-lm.md) | Self-host typed decisions (noul/choice/score) with CLM-8B, rank free-form candidates directly, compare against Jev on the bundled T-Rex example, or fine-tune the head on your own trajectories. | Python · `contrastive-lm` package (`clm-serve`, `CLMClient`), vLLM embedding server (Apache-2.0) |
| [clonemap (finds helpers many Python apps rewrite by hand, labelled with Jev)](clonemap.md) | Research probe that clones a few thousand Django and LLM apps, groups functions that do the same thing with different code, compares them with a control set and lists candidates for a missing library; Jev labels the top clusters and checks proposed libraries. | Python research pipeline and static results explorer (MIT) |
| [codegraph-jev](codegraph-jev.md) | Benchmarks BM25/embeddings/call-graph + Jev judge against a coding agent on code-reading tasks. | Python research harness · TypeSafe Jev (MIT) |
| [College Event Moderator](college-event-moderator.md) | Benchmark and local playground that screens college chapter event proposals with Jev or a local s1ced or Vega model: five shared questions per event, expected answers, and a report of accuracy, confidence, latency and cost. | Python package with CLI, benchmark runner and local dashboard (uv) (Apache-2.0) |
| [Conjevture](conjevture.md) | Combine Boolean rules with declared probability models; convert Jev Noul/Choice answers while keeping provenance (offline example; optional live game). | TypeScript · npm library (MIT) |
| [Creative Decision Making (cdm)](creative-decision.md) | Paper and Python library that combine calibrated decision-model judgments (Jev and peers) with decision theory: calibrate, pool, gate unknowns to verify, and pick options plus bounded bets on new ideas, with a benchmark on 237 real Jev judgments. | Python library + paper (MIT) |
| [Cygnet recipe](cygnet-recipe.md) | Serve a self-hosted `/v1/systemone`-compatible decision endpoint from stock Gemma weights and reproduce the maintainer's JevBench public-set run. | Python · vLLM 0.30.0 + shim (`shim/decision_server.py`) (MIT) |
| [DecideBench](decidebench.md) | Benchmark of decision models (Jev and open alternatives) and LLMs measuring accuracy, cost per task and latency together on 400 contrastive decisions, with Pareto frontiers instead of a single ranking. | Python benchmark, dataset and leaderboard (MIT) |
| [Decis](chaitin-decis.md) | Self-host a Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` API with open Laya/kev engines in Docker (independent of hosted Jev). | Python · Docker inference server (Apache-2.0) |
| [Decision Index (apolinario)](apolinario-decision-index.md) | Reproduce the Decision Index typed-decision benchmark suite locally or as one Hugging Face Job (not affiliated with TypeSafe). | Python · Decision Index kit + HF Jobs (MIT) |
| [Decision Layer Benchmark](decision-layer-benchmark.md) | Cost of agent decision layers on OpenAI/Anthropic/Google vs Jev, rerunnable. | Python · benchmark + report (MIT) |
| [Decision Model Arena](decision-model-arena.md) | Tetris, bomb battle and image questions comparing Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Decision-1 and Laya. | Python + JavaScript · demo arena (MIT) |
| [Decision models on Polish historical gazetteer entries (IHPAN)](ihpan-modele-decyzyjne-test.md) | Compare Jev with TEV1 and Basal on 250 human-labelled Polish gazetteer fragments. | Python · eval scripts + data (Apache-2.0) |
| [Decision models vs small LLMs (Jev, Kev, Laya and Mammoth-1 against cheap LLMs on agent decisions)](decision-models-vs-small-llms.md) | Rerunnable comparison of decision models (Jev 1.14, Kev-4B, Laya, Mammoth-1) with small LLMs on six decisions an agent makes between generative steps: routing, classification, a guardrail, an ordered score and a mixed workflow, scored on accuracy, calibration, format failures, latency and cost. | Python benchmark harness with raw results and reports (MIT) |
| [decision-eval](decision-eval.md) | Benchmark harness for zero-shot decision models (choice / noul / score): accuracy, calibration, latency, and cost across suites, with a hosted Jev backend on OpenRouter (`typesafe/jev-1.14`) alongside local Laya/GLiNER and OpenAI Decisions. | Python CLI (`uvx` / `uv run`) (MIT) |
| [Decision-model referral on medical exams](decision-model-referral.md) | Reproduce a confidence-based referral study of Jev and other decision models on medical licensing questions. | Python · research code + data (MIT code; question data under source licences) |
| [decision-models-eval](decision-models-eval.md) | Independent, pre-registered evaluation of SubQ Snap against TypeSafe Jev 1.13 and 1.14 on Decision Index benchmarks, including a long-context fairness test (whole documents vs chunks), with figures and confidence intervals. | Python evaluation code + results (MIT) |
| [decision-toolkit](decision-toolkit.md) | Local-first toolkit from GIA-UH and Syalia for open-weights System One decision models: typed choice, yes/no, and score questions with calibrated probabilities on CPU, as a library, CLI, and REST server that speaks TypeSafe's System One contract. | Python library + CLI + FastAPI server (MIT) |
| [DecisionBench](decisionbench.md) | Reproduce a Jev-vs-LLM comparison on typed invoice decisions and inspect where they disagree. | TypeScript · harness + dashboard, Postgres (MIT) |
| [decisionsmith](decisionsmith.md) | Use and fine-tune System One models (Jev, Laya) on your own data with an LLM as teacher: declare decisions as Pydantic fields and get a harness that calls Jev or a trained local student. | Python library (`uv add decisionsmith[all]`) and notebooks (Apache-2.0) |
| [Deqio](deqio.md) | Self-host typed noul/choice/shared decisions behind one local API with swappable engines (Kev/Laya/Open-Jev, etc.). | Python · local decision server + UI (MIT) |
| [Docket (pg-jev Phase 0 benchmark)](docket-pg-jev-bench.md) | Design and reproducible Phase 0 benchmark for durable, versioned, calibrated AI decisions in Postgres: measures jev-1.13 through pg-jev on 2,000 real complaint narratives for calibration, determinism and context effects. | Docker harness (Postgres + pg-jev) + design docs (Apache-2.0) |
| [Document classifier (financial document types)](jev-document-classifier.md) | Node app and benchmark that names a financial document's type (10-K, proxy statement, fund fact sheet, trade confirmation and 17 more) with one Jev Choice call, compared on 43 synthetic documents against frontier and small LLMs, a bi-encoder and keyword rules. | Node.js web demo, serverless API route and benchmark scripts (MIT) |
| [dopp](dopp.md) | Proxy Jev-shaped decisions, capture traffic, train a small owned model, and serve hosted/offline/in-browser. | Python/TypeScript · capture + train/serve (MIT) |
| [DriveJev](drivejev.md) | Run/study DriveJev-4B open System I driving decisions (behaviour probabilities) with the JevPilot closed-loop harness. | Python · HF weights + simulator harness (MIT) |
| [English Net (prompt-injection detector of yes/no questions)](english-net.md) | Prompt-injection detector whose seven neurons are English yes/no questions answered by Jev in one call; on a locked confirmation set it ties a single good Jev question on ranking, while every verdict names the questions behind it and a blind spot can be patched by adding one sentence. | Research code with live web demo (Python, Next.js) (MIT) |
| [EuLLM](eullm.md) | Serve Jev-shaped typed decisions locally (up to 64 questions per request) next to ordinary chat endpoints, from an ARM board to data-centre GPUs, with every decision written to an audit trail. | Rust (llama.cpp-based) · single binary engine (AGPL-3.0) |
| [Evaluating and Benchmarking the System One Model Jev](jev-benchmarking.md) | Reproduce a 37-dataset zero-shot benchmark of Jev against open-weight LLMs. | Python · evaluation harness + paper assets (MIT) |
| [finance-decision-evals](finance-decision-evals.md) | Independent ASC 606 revenue-recognition eval of Jev on synthetic contract clauses. | TypeScript · eval + report (MIT) |
| [FuZev](fuzev.md) | Jev-compatible Choice, yes/no, and Score decisions alongside text generation on vLLM/ExLlamaV3 from one loaded model. | Open model + serving notes (Apache-2.0) |
| [game-rsi](game-rsi.md) | Real-game teacher correction and gated model evolution experiments with PlayJev configuration packs. | Python game RSI / PlayJev configs (MIT) |
| [goinfer](goinfer.md) | Run Jev-shaped decisions locally from Go, scoring options with a GGUF model or a JEV-layout decision head, with published measurements of how that compares to a trained head. | Go · library + `goinfer-serve` / `goinfer-chat` binaries (MIT) |
| [Groot 1 (open typed-decision model in the System One format)](groot-1.md) | Open 149M-parameter typed-decision model that answers System One choice, score and noul questions with full probability distributions over states of up to 64k tokens, with a pure Bend implementation of its forward pass; the author reports it beating Jev on their 2,000-question test split. | Python package (PyTorch) with Hugging Face weights and an optional HTTP server (Apache-2.0) |
| [Gut Feel Benchmark](gut-feel-benchmark.md) | Benchmark of snap probabilities at deadlines from a quarter second to 16 seconds: 703 boolean items in four packs (poker equity, base-rate puzzles, heart disease, Hacker News outcomes), sent to Jev, other decision models and chat models through AI SDK's `experimental_decide`. | TypeScript benchmark (Node) with packs, results and report generator (MIT) |
| [gutsy](gutsy.md) | Run gutsy-0.8b local calibrated yes/no/choice/score decisions via Jev-compatible APIs on CPU (independent of hosted Jev). | Python · llama.cpp + HF GGUF (Apache-2.0) |
| [Harness² (recursive agent harnessing)](harness2-recursive-harnessing.md) | Python framework that improves an agent's harness (prompts, guardrails, memory, tools, skills, scripts) task by task while the model weights stay fixed, with OpenCode and Codex executors, three professional benchmarks, and optional Jev selection of the best final output when it runs k > 1 candidates. | Python package and CLI (uv, Python 3.12) with benchmark runners and a web dashboard (Apache-2.0) |
| [Hopwise (knowledge-graph traversal benchmark)](hopwise.md) | Benchmark (README in Vietnamese) where Jev picks which edge to follow at each node of a Neo4j knowledge graph built from Wikidata and SEC EDGAR: 26 of 60 questions right (43%) in a 4.2 s median for $0.07, against 25% for Gemma 4 12B and 27% for gpt-oss-20b. | FastAPI backend and React dashboard over Neo4j, with crawled graph data (Apache-2.0) |
| [Huncho](huncho.md) | Rust server that implements the Jev `/v1/systemone` API for self-hosted System One models (ONNX, Laya, Kev, Clef runtimes, plus a mock), so the TypeSafe SDK can point at your own server. | Rust server/CLI (Cargo; Fedora/EPEL COPR packages) (Apache-2.0) |
| [imajev](imajev.md) | Open multimodal typed-decision models that answer constrained options with probabilities and can't-tell. | Open weights · local serve (Apache-2.0); not TypeSafe-hosted Jev |
| [is-lesion](is-lesion.md) | npm package that evaluates MRI and other medical scan images against Hugging Face dataset labels with Jev 1.14 (Noul, Choice, Score and Box), or local OpenJev vision models, returning a label, probability and bounding box; can score dataset rows directly. | npm package (`is-lesion`) (MIT) |
| [J3v](j3v.md) | Edge-compiled System One decisions (J3v∶Jev :: k3s∶k8s) with Rust compiler and firmware demos. | Rust · compiler/runtime (MIT) |
| [Jarvis (Amphion)](jarvis-amphion.md) | Research system and paper ("Coordination Deserves a Brain of Its Own") for full-duplex voice agents: JEV decides each turn (answer, memory, worker, clarify, control, ignore or wait) so you can keep talking while background tasks run. | Python system + paper (Apache-2.0) |
| [Jeeves (PostHog)](posthog-jeeves.md) | Run a 9B Jev-like reasoning decision model (noul/choice/score) via a Jev-compatible API; independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. | Python · open weights + serving (MIT) |
| [Jeff (firelex)](firelex-jeff.md) | Run Jev-format choice/noul/score decisions locally with a small base model plus task adapters, e.g. in front of a larger local LLM. | Python · `jeff-serve` (uv) · Hugging Face weights / GGUF |
| [Jev + Claude Code case study](jev-claude-code-case-study.md) | Two runnable patterns (Jev inside an app, Jev guarding Claude Code) with offline evals and written research. | Python 3.11+ · `typesafe-sdk` (MIT) |
| [Jev 1.14 comparison benchmark (etsabary)](jev-1-14-comparison-benchmark.md) | 1,136 deterministic text decisions across 25 families run on eleven services (Jev 1.14 and 1.13, Luna Decisions, Luna, Mercury, Gemini Flash-Lite, Qwen, Granite and others) with matched diagnostics, latency percentiles and versioned results. | Python benchmark scripts, data, figures and report (MIT) |
| [Jev Bench (cocodedk)](cocodedk-jev-bench.md) | Classifier workbench: repeatable Jev latency/accuracy runs (Banking77, OpenRouter). | Python · CLI workbench (Apache-2.0) |
| [Jev decision-model benchmarks (vilinski)](vilinski-jev-benchmarks.md) | Reproducible decision-model benchmark: a hand-labelled own set (60 tasks x3) and a 1,100-item public sample, run against Jev and other System One routes, with raw results, pricing snapshots and generators for a published report and interactive model explorer. | Python scripts (standard library) + saved results and HTML report (MIT OR Apache-2.0) |
| [Jev in Large Language Gibbs](jev-gibbs.md) | Replication of the TruthfulQA consistent-reasoning experiment from Large Language Gibbs (arXiv 2606.19264) with Jev supplying per-claim conditionals, plus controls, GSM8K, knights-and-knaves, entity resolution and kinship tests of where Gibbs helps. | Python research scripts with raw results and logs (MIT) |
| [JEV MLX](jev-mlx.md) | Typed decisions from existing MLX-LM and MLX-VLM models on Apple Silicon without fine-tuning or extra decision weights. | Python library (Apple Silicon MLX) (MIT) |
| [Jev Paired-Replay Study](jev-paired-replay.md) | Pre-registered study of whether identical replays flip Jev 1.13's action at shipped thresholds: 12,705 calls on 847 near-threshold items from Banking77, CLINC150 and HelpSteer2 found about 18.5% flips at 0.5 and 0.6 and 5.3% at 0.9, with flips nearly cancelling out. | Python replication data (Parquet), harness and an offline verification script (MIT) |
| [Jev RAG Chinese Benchmark](jev-rag-zh-benchmark.md) | Chinese reranking benchmark (T2Reranking) for Jev Score/Noul with frozen protocol and logs. | Python · benchmark (MIT) |
| [JEV Trading Command Center](jev-trading-command-center.md) | Paper-trading research app (README in Traditional Chinese) where Jev decisions pass validation and risk checks before a simulated broker acts, shown in an animated 3D command center with model 'pets' and an offline demo that uses only synthetic data. | Local Python web app with a Three.js 3D front end (MIT) |
| [Jev Vision Benchmarks](jev-vision-benchmarks.md) | Benchmarks of Jev 1.14 image input on marketplace tasks (product-photo moderation, photo-to-listing matching, classification, package-label reading) against GPT vision models, AWS Rekognition and OpenAI moderation, with checked-in labels and results. | Python benchmark scripts with data and write-ups (No LICENSE file) |
| [Jev vs Basal insurance benchmark](jev-basal-insurance-benchmark.md) | Polish motor-insurance (autocasco) decision benchmark: 325 synthetic typed decisions built from public policy terms, with code-computed labels, run against Jev, Basal 2.0 and Gemini models with accuracy, calibration (ECE) and latency. | Python scripts + dataset (`zestaw.jsonl`) and raw results; README in Polish with English summary (MIT) |
| [Jev vs. LLM (Banking77)](jev-vs-llm-banking77.md) | Small, fair test of the "up to 200x faster" claim: 100 real Banking77 customer messages sorted into 77 categories by Jev, Claude Haiku 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1, measuring accuracy, off-list answers, median and p90 latency, and cost. | TypeScript benchmark script plus a Next.js side-by-side viewer (MIT) |
| [jev-bench (stern9)](stern9-jev-bench.md) | Benchmark harness that asks Jev and general LLMs (via system-one-adapter) the same routing, yes/no and rating questions and reports accuracy, p50/p95 latency, calibration and cost. | Python CLI (MIT) |
| [jev-browsecomp](jev-browsecomp.md) | Measure Jev document screening vs RLM/LLM arms on BrowseComp-Plus with id→span citation checks. | Python · research harness (Apache-2.0) |
| [jev-calibration](jev-calibration.md) | Plot and reproduce Jev calibration (reliability/ECE) on 240 labelled tool-call cases. | Python research scripts (Apache-2.0) |
| [jev-cascade](jev-cascade.md) | Python cascade that uses Jev as a cheap, recall-tuned first stage over many questions per item, then only escalates the uncertain band to a stronger second-stage command and reports whether stage two actually improves accuracy. | Python CLI (stdlib) with recorded demos and a browser explainer (MIT) |
| [jev-click-bench](jev-click-bench.md) | Benchmark of Jev 1.14's `box` tool as a GUI click model on ScreenSpot-v2 and ScreenSpot-Pro with the official scoring: 84% on ordinary screens, 19% on 4K pro apps, and 43% to 60% on Pro after one or two zoomed second looks, with every request logged. | Python benchmark harness with logged runs and analysis (MIT) |
| [jev-context-filter-harness](jev-context-filter-harness.md) | Paper code and run records for screening LLM agents' tool output with Jev: a relevance filter keeps chunks that answer the agent's question and an injection filter withholds planted instructions. | Python research harness (`uv`) with experiment data (MIT) |

| [Waldo Bench](waldo-bench.md) | Can a decision model find Waldo? 66 Where's Waldo scenes cut into tiles and nine image-input decision models asked to pick his tile: Jev 1.14 found 47 (71%) with the fastest typical search, 2.6 s, behind Liquid d1-ultra's 83%. | Python benchmark (uv) with results, leaderboard and per-tile-size curves (MIT) |
| [WaterSheep](watersheep.md) | Run an Apache-2.0 decision model locally behind a Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone`: noul/choice/score plus multi-label answers with a probability per option (independent of hosted Jev). | Python · local server + HF weights + browser demo (Apache-2.0) |

| [YOLO vs Jev: the flying car](yolo-jev.md) | One notebook that runs YOLO26 and Jev 1.14 on the 34 frames of a clip where a car flies over a car show: Jev finds the car in the air in 17 of 19 frames with no false alarms, and names every boxed vehicle, against YOLO's 0 to 4. | Jupyter notebook (uv project) with saved results and an output video (MIT) |
| [zh-decision-bench](codyqin-zh-decision-bench.md) | Chinese-language calibration benchmark for Jev-class System One decision models (dataset CC BY 4.0; code… | Python · HF dataset + eval (Apache-2.0 code; dataset CC BY 4.0) |

| [@rbxts/jev (Jev for roblox-ts)](rbxts-jev.md) | Typed TypeSafe Jev client for roblox-ts games: declare choice, score and noul questions about one state, and the option names become TypeScript types your server code branches on, with a shared rate limiter, retries and request deduplication over Roblox `HttpService`. | roblox-ts package (install from GitHub) (MIT) |
| [adk-go-typesafe](adk-go-typesafe.md) | Call System One from Go and Google ADK-Go with OpenAPI-generated types (Choice/Score/Noul). | Go · module + ADK tool (`adk-go-typesafe`) |

| [anofox-decide](anofox-decide.md) | Evaluate NL predicates/choices in DuckDB SQL via TypeSafe Jev or local open decision models (remote opt-in). | C++ · DuckDB extension (MIT) |

| [Connector for TypeSafe Jev (WordPress)](connector-for-typesafe-jev.md) | WordPress plugin that gives themes and plugins a typed way to ask Jev about content and branch on a probability, a named choice or a score with confidence, with the key managed by WordPress core's Connectors API; a companion plugin backs comment moderation with it. | WordPress plugin (PHP) (GPL-2.0) |

| [paper-sift](paper-sift.md) | Claude Code plugin that builds a reading list for learning a research area: it collects up to 800 candidate papers from Semantic Scholar, screens every abstract with Jev against the survey's rules, reads the best open-access papers, and reports a ranked list. | Claude Code plugin (Python scripts) (MIT) |
| [Parallax (arXiv literature search plugin with Jev screening)](parallax-dsh.md) | DeepSeek Harness plugin that harvests arXiv metadata into local SQLite and lets the agent build auditable paper sets with keyword recall, set operations and Jev screening and classification, where each paper gets an inclusion probability. | DeepSeek Harness (dsh) plugin (TypeScript) (Apache-2.0) |
| [pg-jev](pg-jev.md) | Ask semantic questions from SQL over database rows. | PostgreSQL · PL/Python extension |
| [pg_typesafe](pg-typesafe.md) | Call Choice/Noul/Score from SQL via a C+libcurl extension with batched multi-text helpers (pre-alpha; distinct from pg-jev). | PostgreSQL · C extension |
| [Pi Durable News Agent](pi-durable-news-agent.md) | Proactive pi-durable news agent: a cron tick sweeps about 28 feeds plus topic searches, Jev throws out off-topic or low-value items and sorts the rest into Hype, Facts, and Signal, and research subagents dig into the strongest stories. | TypeScript workspace (pi-durable agent + apps) (MIT) |
| [Pi Workspace Search (bounded code retrieval with optional Jev classification)](pi-workspace-search.md) | Pi extension that gives the agent bounded workspace search: ripgrep for literal and regex queries, a local zvec index for concepts when present, and Jev to classify which code chunks answer a concept query when local results are not enough, keeping output small for the main model. | Pi extension (TypeScript and Node.js) (MIT) |
| [pi-thunder-jev](pi-thunder-jev.md) | `jev_find` for Pi: Jev ranks files and excerpts for a code question. | TypeScript · Pi extension (MIT) |
| [postgres-search](postgres-search.md) | Add a Jev re-rank and 'did you mean' stage to SQL product search, with a published evaluation. | SQL + TypeScript · Postgres search + Node demo server (MIT) |
| [ReadyCode Reader](readycode-reader.md) | Local PDF/DOCX/XLSX evidence for agents: TypeSafe Jev checks passages before cited answers (MCP + browser) | Node.js · MCP (`@readycode/reader`) + browser demo (Apache-2.0) |
| [RepoFinder](repofinder.md) | Codebase map + BM25 search with optional Jev rerank; Claude answers with citations. | Python · CLI (MIT) |
| [Search Gateway](search-gateway.md) | Self-hostable gateway and SDK for 13 AI search providers (Exa, Perplexity, Tavily, Brave and more) with Jev-first auto routing that picks the provider and search breadth, falling back to OpenAI Decisions and then rules. | TypeScript gateway + TS/Python SDKs, React, CLI and MCP (MIT) |
| [searxng-jev-mcp](searxng-jev-mcp.md) | MCP server that gives coding agents one search tool over your own SearXNG: it cleans the query, picks vertical engines, merges duplicates, ranks with BM25F and can rerank results with a Jev-compatible decision model. | Python MCP server (uv) (MIT) |
| [Semble + Jev (semble-jev)](semble-jev.md) | Semble retrieves local snippets; TypeSafe Jev scores relevance; CLI returns original source (`sj`). | Python · CLI via uv (Apache-2.0) |
| [sgrep](sgrep.md) | Semantic grep: chunk a repo and ask TypeSafe Jev which chunks match a plain-English query (mock offline). | Python · CLI (`sgrep`) |
| [sieve](sieve.md) | Local MCP: enumerate repo/search candidates and score with TypeSafe Jev (`jev_grep` / `jev_rank` / `jev_search`). | Python · MCP server (`sieve` 0.1.0, uv) |
| [sift-light](sift-light.md) | Replace an agent's grep/glob with paged, evidence-carrying search; set `semanticJudge.enabled: true` with `provider: "jev"` so Jev classifies hybrid-search candidates. | Node.js 22.19+ · npm package (Pi/OMP plugin, MCP server) (AGPL-3.0) |
| [skimmer](skimmer.md) | Go CLI that splits pages, files or lists into chunks and asks Jev one yes/no relevance question per chunk, keeping only what helps with your goal to save agent tokens. | Go CLI (Homebrew, apt, `go install`) (MIT) |
| [SlopSearX](slopsearx.md) | Serve agent-friendly search (HTML/JSON/YAML, MCP) and let Jev add the specialist engines that can contribute distinctive evidence and reorder a bounded shortlist. | Python · server + MCP (`slopsearx-mcp`), Docker/k8s (MIT) |
| [SuperLocalMemory](superlocalmemory.md) | Store and recall agent memories locally (CLI, dashboard, Claude Code/Codex hooks); opt in to *Online with Jev* so TypeSafe or OpenRouter judges the top 3 results and SLM can say "I don't have that". | Python 3.12+ (npm or pip install) · CLI, daemon, dashboard, MCP/plugin (AGPL-3.0) |
| [sys1grep (jev-semgrep)](jev-semgrep.md) | Filter lines by whether a plain-language proposition holds, with AND/OR/NOT meanings via TypeSafe Jev (not Semgrep Inc). Renamed from jev-semgrep / `@uehaj/semgrep`. | Node.js · CLI (`@uehaj/sys1grep`) |
| [tf-jev](tf-jev.md) | Compile natural-language Text-Fabric corpus queries through typed Jev decisions and a deterministic compiler. | Python · library prototype (MIT) |
| [toolhunch](toolhunch.md) | Choose the right tool from a large MCP/tool catalog with search plus a Jev decision that can abstain. | Python · PyPI `toolhunch` (MIT) |
| [Truffler](truffler.md) | Rails intent search with TypeSafe Jev index-time labels, query understanding, and optional streamed reranking. | Ruby · Rails gem (MIT) |
| [web-scout-ai](web-scout-ai.md) | Read real sources (HTML, JS sites, documents) rather than snippets, with Jev choosing which follow-up links to open and classifying content; GPT fallback available. | Python · PyPI `web-scout-ai` (MIT) |
| [webctl](webctl.md) | Agent web-search CLI: multi-provider results scored/judged (and optionally chunk-scored) with TypeSafe Jev. | Go · CLI (`webctl`) |
| [webular (web research CLI with Jev decisions)](webular.md) | Web research CLI that covers search, scrape, crawl, map, extract, summarize, answer and multi-step research with free, open-source parts, and uses Jev for its judgment calls, with a deterministic fallback for every decision. | CLI and MCP server (Bun, npm `webular`) (Apache-2.0) |
| [XERJ](xerj.md) | Index code, docs, logs and PDFs for agents; add `"rerank": {}` to a `_search` so Jev scores the top hits, or use the local `/v1/systemone`-compatible `_decide` endpoint (answered by XERJ, not Jev). | Rust · single binary (HTTP + MCP), Elasticsearch-compatible API (Apache-2.0) |
| [Zerikai Memory](zerikai-memory.md) | Local code-memory MCP with TypeSafe Jev semantic judgment over retrieved snippets | Python · MCP server (MIT) |

## Try a smaller example

The repository also maintains its own [teaching examples](../../../examples/README.md), [Support Router](../../../projects/support-router/README.md), and [routing evaluation runner](../../../evaluations/README.md). These are useful when you want a small offline starting point before adopting a community project.

## Share or improve a tool

Follow [Add a community project](../../../CONTRIBUTING.md#add-a-community-project) and the [project-page template](../../PROJECT_TEMPLATE.md). Put the full guide in this folder, list it once in a category above, and keep its upstream and guide links in the root README. Corrections to setup instructions and limitations are welcome.

Upstream maintainers own their code and licenses. Check each page's reviewed version and [validation scope](../../../docs/validation.md#community-project-checks); a listing does not establish production quality or endorsement.
